financial-analysis--forgone-discount-trade-credit-effective-annual-cost@r1 :: Effective annual cost of forgoing a trade discount
  kind=definition expr=cost = (1 + discount / (1 - discount)) ** (year / delay) - 1
  original: Effective annual cost = (1 + discount/(1-discount))^(year length/extra credit period) - 1
  source_id=winkler-trade-credit-1996 locator=Traditional Trade Credit Analysis, equation 1
  translation: Year and delay are physical durations in days and their ratio is dimensionless. The selected year length is an input; the rate is a base-one fraction. This is a trade-credit cash-discount application, distinct from nominal-to-effective rate conversion.
  var discount     role=input   qty=early_payment_discount_fraction                         unit=1        dims=dimensionless domain=Finite real scalar
      def: Invoice discount available for early payment
  var year         role=input   qty=annualization_year_duration                             unit=d        dims=time          domain=Finite real scalar
      def: Year length under the selected day-count convention
  var delay        role=input   qty=extra_trade_credit_duration                             unit=d        dims=time          domain=Finite real scalar
      def: Time between discount deadline and full payment deadline
  var cost         role=output  qty=trade_credit_effective_annual_cost                      unit=1        dims=dimensionless domain=Finite real scalar
      def: Annualized cost of forgoing the discount
  guards: ['discount >= 0', 'discount < 1', 'year > 0', 'delay > 0']
  assumptions: ['The discount is the fraction of invoice price and the full invoice is paid at the net due date.', 'The identical extra-credit opportunity is hypothetically compounded over the chosen year.']
  limitations: ['This conventional annualization is not a complete NPV analysis of a specific supplier decision.', 'Actual repayment timing, taxes and financing terms may differ.']
  unresolved: []
  src [project-derivation] cfa-l1v3: CFA Program Curriculum 2023 Level I Volume 3: Financial Statement Analysis, Corporate Issuers
      url=None accessed=2026-11-03
      locator=Corporate Issuers Learning Module 5, Financing Options, printed p764 (PDF p778), Keown Knowledge Check 1
      evidence=The example compounds the forgone discount over the extra credit period to an effective annual cost.
      license=Purchased owner copy; personal-use terms assumed; no redistribution
  src [primary] winkler-trade-credit-1996: The Cost of Trade Credit: A Net Present Value Perspective
      url=https://libres.uncg.edu/ir/uncg/f/d_winkler_cost_1996.pdf accessed=2026-11-03
      locator=Traditional Trade Credit Analysis, equation 1
      evidence=The article gives the conventional effective annual trade-credit cost and discusses limitations of the traditional comparison.
      license=University-hosted author copy; reuse terms not established; formula-level facts and original paraphrase only.
  ref two-ten-net-thirty: {"discount": 0.02, "year": 365, "delay": 20} -> 0.445852927312486 (tol 1e-09)
      method=A 2% discount foregone for 20 additional days implies about 44.6% effective annual cost.
      provenance=Curator-constructed independent numerical check; no empirical validation claimed.
  ref no-discount: {"discount": 0, "year": 360, "delay": 30} -> 0 (tol 1e-09)

financial-analysis--fifo-retained-earnings-from-lifo-reserve@r1 :: FIFO retained earnings from a LIFO reserve
  kind=definition expr=fifo = retained + reserve * (1 - tax)
  original: FIFO retained earnings = LIFO retained earnings + LIFO reserve × (1 - tax rate)
  source_id=wiley-inventory-2011 locator=Reading 16, practice solutions 4, 7 and 8
  translation: The reserve may be signed; tax is a base-one fraction.
  var retained     role=input   qty=lifo_retained_earnings                                  unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: reported LIFO retained earnings
  var reserve      role=input   qty=lifo_inventory_reserve                                  unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: FIFO less LIFO inventory reserve
  var tax          role=input   qty=inventory_adjustment_tax_rate                           unit=1        dims=dimensionless domain=Finite real scalar
      def: Uniform tax rate applicable to the cumulative reserve
  var fifo         role=output  qty=fifo_retained_earnings                                  unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: FIFO retained earnings
  guards: ['tax >= 0', 'tax <= 1']
  assumptions: ['The reserve reflects cumulative FIFO-minus-LIFO differences for the same entity and date.', 'A single tax rate applies to the cumulative adjustment, with all other retained-earnings effects unchanged.']
  limitations: ['Different historical tax rates require an explicit cumulative tax adjustment, as illustrated elsewhere in the reading.', 'This analytical restatement does not itself imply a cash tax payment.']

      def: Estimated annual reporting period count
  guards: ['cost >= 0', 'depreciation > 0']

financial-analysis--asset-taxable-temporary-difference-deferred-liability@r2 :: Deferred tax liability from an asset taxable temporary difference
  kind=definition expr=liability = (carrying - taxbase) * rate
  original: Deferred tax liability = (asset carrying amount - tax base) × applicable tax rate
  source_id=ias12-2022 locator=Paragraphs 15, 24–25 and 47; examples of taxable and deductible differences
  translation: This is an undiscounted liability from one asset; the positive taxable difference is carrying amount less tax base.
  var carrying     role=input   qty=asset_carrying_amount                                   unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: asset carrying amount
  var taxbase      role=input   qty=asset_tax_base                                          unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: asset tax base
  var rate         role=input   qty=deferred_tax_reversal_rate                              unit=1        dims=dimensionless domain=Finite real scalar
      def: Enacted or substantively enacted rate applicable when the difference reverses
  var liability    role=output  qty=asset_deferred_tax_liability                            unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: deferred tax liability
  guards: ['carrying >= taxbase', 'rate >= 0', 'rate <= 1']
  assumptions: ['The temporary difference is taxable on recovery and recognized under the applicable standard; no recognition exception applies.', 'A single enacted or substantively enacted rate applies to the expected recovery.']
  limitations: ['Different tax rates, recovery methods, recognition exceptions and offset rules require separate analysis.', 'Tax effects booked in OCI or equity still require appropriate balance-sheet recognition and allocation.']
  unresolved: []
  src [project-derivation] cfa-l1v3: CFA Program Curriculum 2023 Level I Volume 3: Financial Statement Analysis, Corporate Issuers
      url=None accessed=2026-11-03
      locator=Financial Statement Analysis Learning Module 7, Current and Deferred Tax Assets and Liabilities, printed p411 (PDF p425), unnumbered year-by-year calculation
      evidence=The reading multiplies an asset taxable temporary difference by the applicable tax rate.
      license=Purchased owner copy; personal-use terms assumed; no redistribution
  src [primary] ias12-2022: IAS 12 Income Taxes
      url=https://www.ifrs.org/content/dam/ifrs/publications/pdf-standards/english/2022/issued/part-a/ias-12-income-taxes.pdf?bypass=on accessed=2026-11-03

      def: asset tax base
  var rate         role=input   qty=deferred_tax_reversal_rate                              unit=1        dims=dimensionless domain=Finite real scalar
      def: Enacted or substantively enacted rate applicable when the difference reverses
  var asset        role=output  qty=asset_deferred_tax_asset                                unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: deferred tax asset
  guards: ['taxbase >= carrying', 'rate >= 0', 'rate <= 1']
  assumptions: ['Recognition criteria are met and the full deductible temporary difference can be utilized.', 'One applicable enacted or substantively enacted reversal-period tax rate applies.']
  limitations: ['Recognition is limited by expected taxable profit and any applicable exceptions; valuation allowances or partial recognition are outside this simple profile.', 'Tax law and rate depend on jurisdiction, timing and recovery method.']

financial-analysis--deferred-tax-liability-rate-change-effect@r2 :: Deferred tax liability change from a rate change
  kind=definition expr=change = difference * (new - old)
  original: Change in deferred tax liability = temporary difference × (new tax rate - old tax rate)
  source_id=ias12-2022 locator=Paragraphs 6, 15, 24–25, 47 and 60–62
  translation: Rates are base-one fractions; the sign of output is the change in the liability.
  var difference   role=input   qty=taxable_temporary_difference                            unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: taxable temporary difference
  var new          role=input   qty=new_deferred_tax_rate                                   unit=1        dims=dimensionless domain=Finite real scalar
      def: New enacted or substantively enacted rate for the reversal period
  var old          role=input   qty=old_deferred_tax_rate                                   unit=1        dims=dimensionless domain=Finite real scalar
      def: Previously applicable rate for the reversal period
  var change       role=output  qty=deferred_tax_liability_rate_change                      unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: change in deferred tax liability
  guards: ['difference >= 0', 'new >= 0', 'new <= 1', 'old >= 0', 'old <= 1']
  assumptions: ['The same recognized taxable temporary difference is held fixed for the rate-change comparison.', 'Each rate is enacted or substantively enacted and applicable to the expected reversal.']
  limitations: ['This isolates the rate effect and excludes temporary-difference changes, exchange effects and recognition changes.', 'Presentation in profit or loss versus OCI or equity depends on the underlying transaction.']
  unresolved: []

  original: Tax base = original cost - cumulative tax deductions
  source_id=ias12-2022-tax-base locator=Paragraphs 7–8 and examples

      def: asset tax base
  guards: ['cost >= 0', 'deductions >= 0', 'cost >= deductions']

financial-analysis--straight-line-bond-discount-premium-amortization@r1 :: Straight-line bond discount or premium amortization
  kind=definition expr=amortization = (face - initial) / periods
  original: Signed amortization per period = (face amount - initial carrying amount) / number of periods
  source_id=openstax-accounting-13-2-straight locator=Section 13.2, straight-line bond premium amortization
  translation: The financial-period count is dimensionless. Positive amortization raises a discounted liability; negative amortization lowers a premium liability.
  var face         role=input   qty=bond_face_amount                                        unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: bond face amount
  var initial      role=input   qty=initial_bond_carrying_amount                            unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: initial bond carrying amount
  var periods      role=input   qty=bond_amortization_period_count                          unit=1        dims=dimensionless domain=Finite real scalar
      def: Number of equal-length periods to maturity
  var amortization role=output  qty=bond_discount_premium_amortization_per_period           unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: signed bond amortization
  guards: ['face >= 0', 'initial >= 0', 'periods > 0']
  assumptions: ['A fixed-rate bond remains outstanding for equal-length periods through maturity.', 'No modification, early redemption or other carrying-value change occurs.']
  limitations: ['IFRS requires effective interest; straight-line is a limited US GAAP approximation when results are not materially different.', 'Unequal periods and changing estimates require another schedule.']
  unresolved: []
  src [project-derivation] cfa-l1v3: CFA Program Curriculum 2023 Level I Volume 3: Financial Statement Analysis, Corporate Issuers
      url=None accessed=2026-11-03
      locator=Financial Statement Analysis Learning Module 8, Accounting for Bond Amortisation, printed pp453–454 (PDF pp467–468), Examples 3 and 4 solutions 4
      evidence=The examples allocate the initial discount or premium evenly across five annual periods.
      license=Purchased owner copy; personal-use terms assumed; no redistribution
  src [primary] openstax-accounting-13-2-straight: Principles of Accounting, Volume 1: Financial Accounting
      url=https://openstax.org/books/principles-financial-accounting/pages/13-2-compute-amortization-of-long-term-liabilities-using-the-effective-interest-method accessed=2026-11-03
      locator=Section 13.2, straight-line bond premium amortization
      evidence=The straight-line example divides bond premium by the number of payment periods and subtracts the amortized premium from cash interest.
      license=OpenStax page displays CC BY-NC-SA and additional usage terms; formula-level facts and original paraphrase only.
  ref discount: {"face": 1000000, "initial": 957876, "periods": 5} -> 8424.8 (tol 1e-09)
      method=The 42,124 discount divided by five periods yields positive 8,424.80.
      provenance=Curator-constructed independent numerical check; no empirical validation claimed.
  ref premium: {"face": 1000000, "initial": 1044518, "periods": 5} -> -8903.6 (tol 1e-09)
      method=The 44,518 premium divided by five periods yields signed amortization of -8,903.60.

financial-analysis--bond-interest-expense-from-coupon-and-amortization@r1 :: Bond interest expense from coupon and signed amortization
  kind=definition expr=expense = coupon + amortization
  original: Interest expense = cash coupon + signed bond discount/premium amortization
  source_id=openstax-accounting-13-2-straight locator=Section 13.2, straight-line bond premium amortization
  translation: A discount amortization is positive and a premium amortization negative; all terms use a common currency.
  var coupon       role=input   qty=bond_coupon_payment                                     unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: cash coupon payment
  var amortization role=input   qty=bond_discount_premium_amortization_per_period           unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: signed bond discount or premium amortization
  var expense      role=output  qty=bond_interest_expense                                   unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: bond interest expense
  guards: ['coupon >= 0']
  assumptions: ['Coupon and signed amortization relate to the same bond and period.', 'No other components of interest expense occur.']
  limitations: ['The amortization method must be chosen separately under the applicable reporting framework.', 'This equation does not calculate the coupon or effective periodic rate.']
  unresolved: []

financial-analysis--current-income-tax-from-taxable-profit@r1 :: Current income tax from taxable profit
  kind=definition expr=current = taxable * rate
  original: Current tax = taxable profit × applicable tax rate
  source_id=ifrs-sme-income-tax-2019 locator=Introduction and examples 5–6, current tax calculation
  translation: Taxable profit and current tax are monetary amounts; rate is a base-one fraction.
  var taxable      role=input   qty=taxable_profit                                          unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: taxable profit
  var rate         role=input   qty=current_income_tax_rate                                 unit=1        dims=dimensionless domain=Finite real scalar
      def: Uniform income tax rate for the taxable profit
  var current      role=output  qty=current_income_tax_expense                              unit=USD      dims=currency      domain=Finite real scalar
      def: current income tax for period
  guards: ['taxable >= 0', 'rate >= 0', 'rate <= 1']
  assumptions: ['A single uniform rate applies to the full positive taxable profit for this period.', 'There are no tax credits, minimum taxes, prior-period adjustments or other tax components.']
  limitations: ['Current tax expense is not necessarily the ending tax payable: prepayments, refunds and prior balances must be considered.', 'Tax-loss carryforwards, tiered rates and jurisdiction-specific rules need additional treatment.']
  unresolved: []
  src [project-derivation] cfa-l1v3: CFA Program Curriculum 2023 Level I Volume 3: Financial Statement Analysis, Corporate Issuers
      url=None accessed=2026-11-03

      evidence=The example multiplies taxable income by the applicable tax rate to determine the period’s current tax.
      license=Purchased owner copy; personal-use terms assumed; no redistribution
  src [primary] ifrs-sme-income-tax-2019: IFRS for SMEs Supporting Material, Module 29: Income Tax
      url=https://www.ifrs.org/content/dam/ifrs/supporting-implementation/smes/module-29.pdf accessed=2026-11-03
      locator=Introduction and examples 5–6, current tax calculation
      evidence=The tax rate is applied to taxable profit to determine current tax, subject to applicable tax rules and payments.
